data-testid=\"game-show-content\"><div class=\"px-3.75 mt-4\"><h2>WHAT IS CRAZY TIME?</h2><p>Crazy Time by Evolution is one of India's most popular online casino game shows. The game has a Money Wheel, a Top Slot for added multipliers, and four exciting bonus games; Cash Hunt, Pachinko, Coin Flip, and the main featured game, Crazy Time. It is widely available at online casinos serving Indian players and can be played on both desktop and mobile devices.</p><h2>THE CRAZY TIME MONEY WHEEL</h2><p>The Crazy Time Money Wheel is similar to the spin wheels used in games like Dream Catcher and Monopoly Live, but with a different set of segments: 1 (1x), 2 (2x), 5 (5x), 10 (10x), Pachinko, Cash Hunt, Coin Flip, and Crazy Time. All bonus segments can pay up to ₹4 crore (approx. €500,000) per round, depending on multipliers and game outcomes. The game outcomes are generated using certified game technology from Evolution, ensuring fair and independent results for players in India and worldwide.</p><h2>THE TOP SLOT</h2><p>The Top Slot is a Random Number Generator (RNG) feature that generates one random multiplier for a random bet spot. The multiplier can apply to either a number or a bonus game. For example, if the Top Slot assigns a x5 multiplier to the \"10\" segment, that segment will pay out 50x. History shows that it can be very valuable when the wheel lands on a spot that the Top Slot has generated a multiplier for, especially when it's on one of the four bonus games.</p><h2>CASH HUNT</h2><p>The Cash Hunt is a bonus game played on a gallery of 108 different multipliers. All multipliers are shown and then hidden behind various symbols. Once the host pulls the golden lever, all the symbols such as stars, joker hats, parcels, rabbits, cactuses, cupcakes, and chickens are scrambled. When the scrambling stops, you have to select the symbol you believe is hiding the largest multiplier. When the countdown comes to an end, the gallery will reveal the multiplier hidden behind your selected symbol. The multiplier will be added to your initial bet amount.</p><h2>COIN FLIP</h2><p>The Coin Flip bonus game works exactly like a simple coin toss. The RNG feature assigns two different multipliers; one for red and one for blue. The host flips the coin on a launch pad and the colour facing represents the multiplier added to your initial bet to determine the prize you win.</p><h2>PACHINKO</h2><p>Pachinko features a large vertical board with multiple pegs. A ball bounces on the pegs going downwards. The bottom row of the wall is made up of 16 landing zones, with an assigned multiplier. The host releases the ball in a spot decided by RNG on top of the wall landing on one of the 16 multipliers. The multiplier it lands on will be added to your initial bet and determine the prize you win.</p><h2>CRAZY TIME BONUS GAME</h2><p>The Crazy Time bonus game is the bonus the game is named after. There are three flappers on the wheel in this game and smaller multipliers and bonus games are replaced by higher multipliers and the segments \"Double\" and \"Triple\". You can win the bonus round by correctly guessing which flapper will eventually land on the highest multiplier.</p><p>If the colour you have selected lands on \"Double\" or \"Triple\", all multiplier segments will be doubled or tripled, and you get to spin again. The multiplier your selected colour eventually lands on will be added to your initial bet and determine the prize you win. Like all Evolution live casino games available in India, Crazy Time uses audited systems to ensure fairness and transparency.</p><h2>Evolution Gaming Introducing Crazy Time A</h2><p>Due to the massive popularity of Crazy Time, Evolution gaming decided to create a brand new studio. This new studio is called Crazy Time A and offers the exact same game as the original Crazy Time. As a result, you will now notice multiple Crazy Time games available in casino lobbies, allowing even more players to enjoy the thrilling and entertaining gameplay that Crazy Time has to offer. We're tracking the game separately as well on our <a href=\"/india/casinoscores/crazy-time-a/\" class=\"text-corg-foreground underline\">Crazy Time A Stats and tracking page</a></p></div></div></div></div><section data-testid=\"tracking-page-faqs\" class=\"max-md:bg-corg-x-light-bg pb-10\"><div id=\"\" data-testid=\"\" class=\"ml-auto mr-auto w-full max-w-350 The game outcomes are generated using certified game technology from Evolution, ensuring fair and independent results for players in India and worldwide.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\u003ch2\\u003eTHE TOP SLOT\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003cp\\u003eThe Top Slot is a Random Number Generator (RNG) feature that generates one random multiplier for a random bet spot. The multiplier can apply to either a number or a bonus game. For example, if the Top Slot assigns a x5 multiplier to the \\\"10\\\" segment, that segment will pay out 50x. History shows that it can be very valuable when the wheel lands on a spot that the Top Slot has generated a multiplier for, especially when it's on one of the four bonus games.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\u003ch2\\u003eCASH HUNT\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003cp\\u003eThe Cash Hunt is a bonus game played on a gallery of 108 different multipliers. Once the host pulls the golden lever, all the symbols such as stars, joker hats, parcels, rabbits, cactuses, cupcakes, and chickens are scrambled. When the scrambling stops, you have to select the symbol you believe is hiding the largest multiplier. When the countdown comes to an end, the gallery will reveal the multiplier hidden behind your selected symbol. The multiplier will be added to your initial bet amount.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\u003ch2\\u003eCOIN FLIP\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003cp\\u003eThe Coin Flip bonus game works exactly like a simple coin toss. The RNG feature assigns two different multipliers; one for red and one for blue. The host flips the coin on a launch pad and the colour facing represents the multiplier added to your initial bet to determine the prize you win.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\u003ch2\\u003ePACHINKO\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003cp\\u003ePachinko features a large vertical board with multiple pegs. A ball bounces on the pegs going downwards. The bottom row of the wall is made up of 16 landing zones, with an assigned multiplier. The host releases the ball in a spot decided by RNG on top of the wall landing on one of the 16 multipliers. The multiplier it lands on will be added to your initial bet and determine the prize you win.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\u003ch2\\u003eCRAZY TIME BONUS GAME\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003cp\\u003eThe Crazy Time bonus game is the bonus the game is named after. The multiplier your selected colour eventually lands on will be added to your initial bet and determine the prize you win.
